The page tells them that first, in the hero, with one button. Everything else on the page (adaptive drill, the builder, the question jump, insights) is the fallback for a student with nothing assigned, or the second thing after the assignment. Product principle 2 (compute the next step, do not present a grid) is the whole layout brief.\r\n10:\r\n11:Order of the page, top to bottom: utilities (jump, search, LR/RC), the hero (greeting, Next up, one CTA, one quiet alternative, the builder console), the practice card (insights, Analytics, Journal), Today (the queue behind the hero\'s item).\r\n12:\r\n13:## The ground\r\n14:\r\n15:The stage is cream (`--classroom-drill-page`). The cards on it are near-black (`--observatory-surface-card` `#08080b`). One card, the hero, carries a photograph (the horizon), and it is the only decoration on the page. Today was flattened to the same near-black surface as the practice card on 2026-09-08; two photographs on one screen read as a template. No accent colour. No gradients. No grid textures.\r\n16:\r\n17:Controls that sit on the stage itself (the jump row, the section switch) are inked for the stage: `--observatory-stage-*`. Controls on a card are inked ivory: the `--observatory-ink*` ladder. The 2026-09-08 audit found the jump row inked for the cards while sitting on the stage, at 1.07:1. Tone is decided by what a control sits on, never by what is near it.\r\n18:\r\n19:## Ink and contrast\r\n20:\r\n21:WCAG AA is a defect gate here as everywhere. Measured against `#08080b`, the ivory ladder passes for text from `--observatory-ink-50` (4.9:1) upward. The steps below it (0.38, 0.42, 0.45, 0.48) were removed on 2026-09-08; they were carrying labels at 3.2:1 to 4.2:1. Nothing that carries text goes below `ink-50`. Hairlines, rings and skeleton bars use their own tokens and may be fainter.\r\n22:\r\n23:On the stage: `--observatory-stage-ink` 12.9:1, `--observatory-stage-muted` 5.3:1 on `--observatory-stage-well`.\r\n24:\r\n25:Over the photographs, text sits on a scrim (`::before` on the hero and on Today\'s inner surface). The scrim is mandatory; the photographs have bright crops.\r\n26:\r\n27:**The ink ladder does not hold over a photograph, and the contrast scan cannot see it.** The ladder\'s alpha steps were measured against the flat card `#08080b`. The scan reads the scrim\'s declared colour, not the image underneath, so it reported zero fails while three hero lines were failing. Measured on 2026-09-09 by sampling `hub-horizon.webp` under each text box and compositing photo, scrim and the text\'s own alpha: the quiet link was **1.89:1**, the Next up facts line **2.76:1**, the dateline **4.50:1**, all needing 4.5. Two fixes, together: hero text that carries meaning is full ivory (`--observatory-ink`, no alpha step), and the horizontal scrim\'s middle stop went 0.35 to 0.58. That is the measured floor; it clears every hero line and still opens the middle enough for the horizon to read. Any change to the hero photo, the scrim, or hero text colour has to be re-measured the same way, not scanned.\r\n28:\r\n29:**The rule is the greeting column, not a list of lines, and it is measured at every width.** The first pass named the three lines it had measured, which left the rest of the column on the ladder: the assigned hint at **3.24:1** at 375, the paragraphs the failed and empty heroes render, and the explainer. The reason the gap survived review is that `background: cover` at `50% 78%` changes which part of the photograph lands under the text as the hero narrows. The bright horizon is dragged into the greeting column at 375, 390 and 768 and pushed out of it from 1024 up, so the same line passes on a laptop and fails on a phone. One crop never speaks for the others. Every line inside `.drill-observatory-greeting` is now full ivory, the CSS lists the whole ladder rather than the steps in use, and the six widths are the unit of measurement. The console card beside the column keeps the ladder, because it carries its own `rgba(6,6,9,0.55)` panel and measures 8.81:1.\r\n30:\r\n31:**A second scan blind spot, on the default stage.** `bgOf` walks ancestors, so text on an absolutely positioned **sibling** is measured against whatever sits behind that sibling. The section switch\'s active label reports 1.06:1 and is really 11.79:1 on the pill it sits on. Text over a photograph, a gradient, a sibling or a `::before` needs sampling. A 1a fail on this page is a question, not an answer.\r\n32:\r\n33:## Type\r\n34:\r\n35:Three families, on purpose, one job each:\r\n36:\r\n37:- Fraunces, weight 400, `opsz` 60: the greeting only (`h1`). It is the one serif line on the page. Insight values and Today titles use it at 20 to 22px as the page\'s second display voice. Nothing else.\r\n38:- JetBrains Mono, 10 to 11px, tracked 0.16 to 0.18em, uppercase: micro-labels and chips. Never a sentence.\r\n39:- Outfit: everything else. Body 13.5 to 14px, the Next up title 19px semibold, the console heading fluid.\r\n40:\r\n41:## Depth\r\n42:\r\n43:Hairline rings (`0 0 0 1px`) separate surfaces. Shadows are quiet: `0 12px 32px` at 12 to 16 percent, not the 60px at 25 to 35 percent the page shipped with. Glass appears only on the hero console. It is the one `backdrop-filter` on the page, at `blur(12px)`, and it is there because it sits over the photograph. A glass panel anywhere else, including on Today, is a defect.\r\n44:\r\n45:## Controls\r\n46:\r\n47:- 44px minimum by height, met by the control: the CTA (46px), the console button (46px), the section switch options (44px), selects (44px), secondary links and insight actions (44px). No pseudo-element hit areas.\r\n48:- Focus: a 2px solid ivory outline (`--observatory-focus`), offset 3px, on every pill control. Full-bleed rows inside a clipped container use the inset variant (`.drill-observatory-focus-inset`). On the stage, a 2px `--observatory-stage-ink` outline. No control removes an outline without one of these.\r\n49:- Hover: a brightness or colour change. Rails and tiles may lift 2px; the primary CTA never moves.\r\n50:- Motion: `--observatory-motion` (160ms) for state; the section switch\'s indicator is a spring. Transform and opacity only. The RC flip\'s height snaps. `prefers-reduced-motion` stops everything.\r\n51:- The curve is ease-out (`--observatory-ease`, quart), never ease-in-out. A control answering a pointer has to leave immediately: the standard curve covers 2.6% of the distance in the first 10% of a 160ms transition, the quart covers 34.7%. No bounce, no elastic, anywhere.\r\n52:- A transition list names every property its own `:hover` and `:active` change. Three of them named only `transform` and got their background animated by accident, through a Tailwind utility that outranked the rule; once the rules won on specificity those hovers would have snapped. Adding a hover means checking the list.\r\n53:- Every control answers the pointer. The three jump-row selects had `:disabled` and `:focus-visible` and nothing for hover; they now strengthen their ring, mixed from `--qf-ink` so it reads in both tones.\r\n54:\r\n55:## When it fails\r\n56:\r\n57:An empty hero and a failed hero are not the same thing, and the page used to draw them the same way: `primary` is null on error, so a student whose assignments failed to load was told, silently, that she had none. The hero now says what did not load before it offers her own practice, and the Today card says the same for its queue. Both carry a Try again.\r\n58:\r\n59:That button reloads the page. Today is assembled from seven sources and only two are react-query backed, so invalidating queries would retry two of seven and look like it had retried all of them. A reload is the honest action until those hooks are converted.\r\n60:\r\n61:## Help\r\n62:\r\n63:One explainer, on the hero, only for the student with nothing assigned: a `<details>` saying how the adaptive drill picks questions. It quotes no set size, because adaptive mode draws from the whole bank and serves one question at a time (`Drill.tsx`, `mode === \'adaptive\'`); there is no fixed count to quote and inventing one would be worse than omitting it.\r\n64:\r\n65:`MainhubHelpControl` exists in the codebase and is rendered nowhere. Help reaches Joshua through **Messages** in the classroom rail. Do not add a floating help button to the hub to fix this; either mount the control deliberately or leave the rail to carry it.\r\n66:\r\n67:## Keys\r\n68:\r\n69:Escape leaves an open stage and does nothing on the plain hub. It stands down while anything modal is open (the search panel, a Radix popover or select) and while the student is inside a field they have typed into, because Escape is the browser\'s own revert there. Cancel stays the visible way out; the key is the accelerator.\r\n70:\r\n71:The search opens on Cmd/Ctrl+K and on `/`. `/` only opens, never toggles: `/` with the panel open is a character someone is trying to type. Neither is advertised on the trigger, which carries `aria-keyshortcuts` and a `title` instead. Joshua removed the visible badge on 2026-09-08.\r\n72:\r\n73:Section is URL state, remembered per browser in `lsatu.drill.section`, and a stated `?section=` always outranks the memory. Opening a stage pushes a history entry, so Back returns to the hub in the section the student came from.\r\n74:\r\n75:## Doors\r\n76:\r\n77:One door to the builder: the hero console. The practice card does not repeat it. An insight may offer a contextual action (open analytics, open the journal, drill a weak type) because it is a response to real data, not a menu item.\r\n78:\r\n79:While a stage (builder, analytics) is open, the hero is not rendered. The stage carries its own title and Cancel.\r\n80:\r\n81:## Copy\r\n82:\r\n83:No em-dashes anywhere a student can read, including placeholders. Buttons say what they do ("Start", "Resume drill", "Set up an LR drill"). The date reads the same as Classroom Home: `Tuesday · September 8 · 2026`.\r\n84:\r\n85:A button carries the outcome, never the feature name and never the screen name. "Smart Drill" is the product\'s word for the adaptive set and it stays in the sentence that explains it, not on the control: the button says "Practice your weakest types". Same rule retired "Open analytics" for "See where you lose points" and "Open journal" for "Review your missed questions". The second option is offered with "Or", never "instead", which tells a student she declined the assigned work simply by reading past it. Full list and reasons in `wiki/COPY-CORRECTIONS.md` under 2026-09-09.\r\n86:\r\n87:## Known debt (not fixed on 2026-09-08)\r\n88:\r\n89:- Observatory rules are scoped under `.drill-observatory-root` and win by specificity, not `!important`. Run `node scripts/observatory-specificity.mjs` after adding a rule to the block; `--check` fails if anything is unscoped. The four `!important` that remain are in the `prefers-reduced-motion` block, where the override has to beat a later animation rule and specificity cannot promise that. Rules for portalled content (`.drill-observatory-launcher-panel`) live outside the `/* end observatory */` marker on purpose: they render outside the root and must not be scoped to it.\r\n90:- The hub ignores the app\'s dark toggle: the stage is cream either way. Acceptable while the stage is documented as cream here.\r\n91:- `.drill-observatory-practice-tile` and `PracticeTile` are still exported for the RC flip and are no longer used on the LR default stage.\r\nFILE COMPREHENSIVEAUDIT.md\r\n700:\r\n701:### M85. inferTypeFromStem passes regex source strings to String.includes, so several qtype inferences can never match\r\n702:**File:** `src/lib/questionLoader.ts:625-640`  |  **Category:** bug  |  **Subsystem:** lib-core\r\n703:\r\n704:**Problem:** These branches use literal regex syntax inside `.includes()`: line 625 `lower.includes(\'most (strongly )?supported\')`, line 630 `lower.includes(\'main (point|conclusion)\')` and `\'expresses the (main )?conclusion\'`, line 640 `\'claim.*figures\'`, line 661 `\'point.*issue\'`. `.includes` does substring matching, so none of these ever match a real stem. Consequences: \'Which one of the following most accurately expresses the main conclusion\' stems with corrupted/missing questionType fall through (likely mislabeled \'Strengthen\' via includes(\'supports\') at line 604, or \'Unknown\'), and Most Strongly Supported inference only fires on \'most justifies\'. This skews qtype normalization, the manifest, and the adaptive engine\'s per-type mastery.\r\n705:\r\n706:**Fix plan:** Replace the pseudo-regex strings with real checks: `/(main point|main conclusion|expresses the (main )?conclusion)/.test(lower)` for Main Conclusion (and move it ABOVE the Strengthen check, since MC stems contain no strengthen words but \'supports\' ordering is fragile), `/most (strongly )?supported/.test(lower)` for MSS, `/claim.*figures/.test(lower)` for Role, `/point at issue|point.*issue/.test(lower)` for Agree/Disagree. Add unit tests in questionLoader.test.ts with real LSAT stems for each branch.\r\n707:\r\n708:### M86. Adaptive engine hydration failure is permanent for the session and only console-logged\r\n709:**File:** `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:139-201`  |  **Category:** error-handling  |  **Subsystem:** lib-core\r\n710:\r\n711:**Problem:** loadHydrationFromDB on error sets `this.hydrated = true` and status \'failed\' with only console.warn/console.error. ensureHydratedFromDB early-returns when status === \'failed\', so a single transient network blip means the engine runs the whole session with zero history: cooldowns empty (immediate repeats allowed), ability defaults, cold-start behavior, with no retry and no signal to the UI beyond a status getter that callers must poll.\r\n712:\r\n713:**Fix plan:** 1) On failure, do NOT set hydrated = true; leave status \'failed\' but allow ensureHydratedFromDB to retry (e.g. track lastFailureAt and retry after a short backoff, or remove the failed short-circuit so the next call retries). 2) Propagate the failure: have ensureHydratedFromDB reject (or return the status) so the calling hook can surface a toast/banner (\'personalization unavailable, drilling generic set\').\r\n714:\r\n715:### M87. generateSmartDrill masks DB query failure as cold start with a misleading student-facing message\r\n716:**File:** `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:839-857`  |  **Category:** ux-correctness  |  **Subsystem:** lib-core\r\n717:\r\n718:**Problem:** If the attempts query errors, the code takes the same branch as \'fewer than 10 attempts\' and returns a random shuffle with explanation \'Not enough history yet. Drilling a calibration set to learn your baseline.\' A student with 500 attempts whose query failed is told they have no history; the error object is never logged or surfaced anywhere.\r\n719:\r\n720:**Fix plan:** Split the branches: if `error`, throw (or return a result with an honest explanation like \'Could not load your history, serving a general set\' and log/surface the error to the caller for a toast). Keep the calibration message only for the genuine attemptCount < minAttemptsForAnalysis case.\r\n721:\r\n722:### M88. filterQuestionPool discards repeat-allowed questions whenever any unseen question exists\r\n723:**File:** `src/lib/questionPoolService.ts:176-202`  |  **Category:** bug  |  **Subsystem:** lib-core\r\n724:\r\n725:**Problem:** With allowRepeats=true, previously seen questions are pushed to `available` in the forEach (line 183-184). But the final pool assembly then overwrites: if preferUnseen && unseen>0 → available = unseen; else if unseen>0 || recycled>0 → available = [...unseen, ...recycled], dropping every repeat-allowed seen question collected earlier. The `available = questions` fallback only fires when unseen AND recycled are both empty. So allowRepeats with preferUnseen=false behaves identically to no-repeats until the pool is fully exhausted, shrinking the effective pool contrary to the user\'s setting.\r\n726:\r\n727:**Fix plan:** Restructure: compute unseen/recycled/seen buckets, then: if allowRepeats && !preferUnseen → available = questions; if allowRepeats && preferUnseen → available = unseen.length ? unseen : questions; if !allowRepeats → available = preferUnseen && unseen.length ? unseen : [...unseen, ...recycled]. Add unit tests for the four setting combinations.\r\n728:\r\n729:### M89. Milestone achievements require exact question-count equality and never fire if the count is skipped; N+1 existence queries per attempt\r\n730:**File:** `src/lib/achievementEngine.ts:67-105`  |  **Category:** bug  |  **Subsystem:** lib-core\r\nFILE COMPREHENSIVEAUDIT.md\r\n1090:**File:** `src/lib/wajService.ts:63-107`  |  **Category:** data-integrity  |  **Subsystem:** lib-core\r\n1091:\r\n1092:**Problem:** logWrongAnswer reads the row, appends to history_json in JS, and writes back. Two overlapping calls (double-submit, two tabs) lose one history item and miscompute revisit_count. The insert branch also lacks the unique-violation (23505) race handling that ensureJournalEntry (line 172) explicitly implements, so a race between logWrongAnswer and ensureJournalEntry throws instead of retrying as an update.\r\n1093:\r\n1094:**Fix plan:** 1) Handle error.code === \'23505\' on the insert by retrying the update path once. 2) Longer term, move the append to an RPC using `history_json = history_json || $1::jsonb` and `revisit_count = jsonb_array_length(...) - 1` for atomicity.\r\n1095:\r\n1096:### L135. Question bank silently drops section files on non-OK fetch responses\r\n1097:**File:** `src/lib/questionLoader.ts:742-745`  |  **Category:** error-handling  |  **Subsystem:** lib-core\r\n1098:\r\n1099:**Problem:** In doLoad, `if (!response.ok) return null;` skips a file with no logging at all (the catch only covers thrown fetch/JSON errors). If a data file is missing from a deploy (the JSON_FILES list is hand-maintained, ~190 entries), whole PT sections vanish from the bank silently and the manifest just shows fewer questions. There is also no signal when the total loaded count drops to zero besides an info console.log.\r\n1100:\r\n1101:**Fix plan:** Log (at minimum console.error with filename and status) on !response.ok, count failed files, and if failures > 0 expose that on the manifest (e.g. manifest.failedFiles) so admin surfaces can show a warning; consider throwing if zero files load.\r\n1102:\r\n1103:### L136. Biased shuffle via sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5) in smart drill assembly\r\n1104:**File:** `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:849 and 930`  |  **Category:** bug  |  **Subsystem:** lib-core\r\n1105:\r\n1106:**Problem:** Both the cold-start shuffle (`[...pool].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5)`) and the bucket `pick` helper use the comparator-random idiom, which produces a non-uniform shuffle (heavily order-biased in V8\'s TimSort). Questions early in the pool array (lowest PT files first) are systematically over-selected in calibration sets and bucket fills. A correct Fisher-Yates already exists in the same codebase (shuffleArray in questionLoader.ts:917) and is even used at line 968 of this file.\r\n1107:\r\n1108:**Fix plan:** Import shuffleArray from \'./questionLoader\' and replace both `.sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5)` occurrences with `shuffleArray(...)`.\r\n1109:\r\n1110:### L137. detectStimulusLengthSensitivity is a permanent no-op and fatigue message hardcodes sessions[0] length\r\n1111:**File:** `src/lib/drillIntelligence.ts:250-254 and 241`  |  **Category:** dead-code  |  **Subsystem:** lib-core\r\n1112:\r\n1113:**Problem:** detectStimulusLengthSensitivity always returns null (comment admits it), yet is called on every detectPatterns run and its pattern type is part of the public LearningPattern union, implying a capability that does not exist. Separately, the fatigue message uses `sessions[0].length / 2` (the first session\'s length) rather than an average of the sessions that actually showed fatigue, so the \'after N questions\' number can be arbitrary (e.g. from a 2-question first session).\r\n1114:\r\n1115:**Fix plan:** Either implement stimulus-length analysis or delete the method, its call site, and the \'stimulus_length\' union member. For fatigue, compute the average length of fatigued sessions (or median session length) and use Math.floor(avg/2) in the message.\r\nFILE docs/specs/2026-07-28-drill-reasoning-observatory.md\r\n280:\r\n281:A 48–56px band across the top contains:\r\n282:\r\n283:- `DRILL / LOGICAL REASONING` as a small technical route label;\r\n284:- the existing localized date;\r\n285:- the existing personalized welcome;\r\n286:- a quiet readiness label that may only describe real loading state, never\r\n287:  fabricated telemetry.\r\n288:\r\n289:This band uses alignment and a single soft divider, not a nested card.\r\n290:\r\n291:#### Focus bay\r\n292:\r\n293:The left 60–64% is the focus bay.\r\n294:\r\n295:- Smart Drill is the dominant computed action.\r\n296:- The button label and launch behavior remain `Start Smart Drill`.\r\n297:- The Spline robot remains the only rich visual object and is staged at a\r\n298:  smaller, more deliberate scale.\r\n299:- The robot occupies no more than 42% of the primary instrument\'s total area.\r\n300:- The robot\'s mask must feather into the aperture so it feels present within\r\n301:  the material.\r\n302:- The existing Spline fallback remains graceful and non-blocking.\r\n303:- No fake score, streak, energy, or readiness metric may be added.\r\n304:\r\n305:The welcome copy sits on the same baseline system as the CTA. The scene should\r\n306:read as one purposeful invitation, not a greeting column beside an illustration.\r\n307:\r\n308:#### Console bay\r\n309:\r\n310:The right 36–40% is a fixed control recess separated by a tonal shift and one\r\nFILE docs/specs/2026-07-28-drill-reasoning-observatory.md\r\n580:\r\n581:Every region must receive an explicit visual treatment for these states.\r\n582:\r\n583:| Surface | Required states |\r\n584:|---|---|\r\n585:| Search | idle, hover, focus, typing, loading-disabled, results, no results |\r\n586:| Advanced | closed, open, filters active, clear available |\r\n587:| Each select | placeholder, hover, focus, open, selected, dependent-disabled, loading-disabled |\r\n588:| Start | disabled, ready, hover, focus, loading/navigation |\r\n589:| Smart Drill | idle, hover, focus, navigation |\r\n590:| Permission actions | available, loading-permission, locked |\r\n591:| Insight | each of five slides, auto-play, hover-paused, dot-selected |\r\n592:| Today | loading, error, empty, primary focus, supporting item, expanded |\r\n593:| Analytics | loading, error, no data, partial data, populated, active tab, disabled tab |\r\n594:| Spline | loading fallback, ready, failed/non-blocking |\r\n595:\r\n596:Color alone cannot distinguish any state.\r\n597:\r\n598:## Focus choreography\r\n599:\r\n600:The intended eye path changes with the workflow.\r\n601:\r\n602:1. **Entry:** Smart Drill action, then Drill console, then Today.\r\n603:2. **Search focus:** search field and result layer become the local visual apex;\r\n604:   robot and greeting recede.\r\n605:3. **Filter configuration:** the dependent selector trace becomes prominent.\r\n606:4. **Question ready:** Start becomes the hottest element in the console.\r\n607:5. **Permission locked:** the unavailable action stays visible but recedes; the\r\n608:   page must not appear broken.\r\n609:6. **Analytics open:** Analytics heading and active view bar become the apex;\r\n610:   the primary instrument remains present but quieter.\r\n611:7. **Error:** the affected region owns the error. The whole page does not turn\r\n612:   red.\r\n613:\r\n614:## Functional invariant matrix\r\n615:\r\n616:No row may change during implementation.\r\n617:\r\n618:| Contract | Current authority | Verification |\r\n619:|---|---|---|\r\n620:| canonical landing route | `/classroom/drill` | direct load and history |\r\n621:| old route behavior | `/mainhub` redirects | route probe |\r\n622:| Smart Drill launch | `/drill`, adaptive mode, current `returnTo` | inspect location state |\r\n623:| unified builder entry | every LR set-up action opens `stage=type-drill` | URL/history probe |\r\n624:| filter semantics | OR within facets, AND across facets | unit and interaction tests |\r\n625:| independent exact filters | PT, Section, and Q# never reset one another | interaction test |\r\n626:| contextual options | hide unselected zero matches; retain selected values | unit and interaction tests |\r\n627:| launch materialization | preview/manual selection launches exact QIDs | interaction and runner tests |\r\n628:| permission gates | `useUserPermissions` flags | permitted and restricted fixtures |\r\n629:| Analytics URL state | `stage=analytics&tab=…` | URL/history probe |\r\n630:| insight behavior | five slides, seven seconds, hover pause, dots | timer/interaction test |\r\n631:| Today data | existing hooks, merge, dedupe, priority | existing tests and fixture matrix |\r\n632:| Today actions | Start/Continue/Reply/Resume/Review/Practice/Open | target audit |\r\n633:| local hero state | preserved while embedded Analytics changes | browser probe |\r\n634:| rail identity | same mounted rail during Classroom route changes | DOM identity probe |\r\n635:| official drill | no visual or interaction changes | frozen-file diff and visual baseline |\r\nFILE docs/adr/0016-a-drill-link-names-a-run-it-does-not-contain-one.md\r\n1:# 0016 — A drill link names a run, it does not contain one\r\n2:\r\n3:Date: 2026-09-01\r\n4:Status: Accepted\r\n5:\r\n6:## Context\r\n7:\r\n8:Seventeen places start a drill. Nine build a URL; eight hand the drill to\r\n9:`/drill` in `location.state` and put nothing in the address bar. Refresh one of\r\n10:those eight and the drill is gone: a saved set, a Smart Drill, a Testing Centre\r\n11:section. `ptRun` is worse than gone — it never went through the resolver at all\r\n12:(`Drill.tsx:202`), so a refreshed practice-test leg loses the run id it needs to\r\n13:write its close-out, and that section can never be marked finished.\r\n14:\r\n15:`getResolvedDrillRouteState` reads both transports into one shape but only one\r\n16:way. Nothing turns a launch back into a URL, so the URL grammar is whatever each\r\n17:caller happened to build, and the two can disagree: several links carry an\r\n18:assignment id *and* a copy of that assignment\'s question list.\r\n19:\r\n20:The obvious fix is to put the questions in the URL. That is the wrong fix. There\r\n21:is already a durable per-student Run Record holding the ordered question list,\r\n22:every attempt, the position and the clock spent, and on resume the runner adopts\r\n23:that stored queue whole rather than re-shuffling (`Drill.tsx:786`). Refresh\r\n24:already returns the identical run. The only thing refresh destroys is the app\'s\r\n25:ability to work out *which run the student was in*.\r\n26:\r\n27:Separately, `mode` is not the kind. Six materially different things all launch as\r\n28:`mode=type-drill`, told apart only by which extra flag rides along\r\n29:(`guidedAssignmentId`, `assignmentId`, `guidedSetId`, `practiceSlug`, `present`).\r\n30:\r\n31:## Decision\r\n32:\r\n33:A **Drill Launch** is carried in the URL and nowhere else, and it carries the\r\n34:*name* of a run, not its contents. `location.state` stops being a transport.\r\n35:\r\n36:The launch is one discriminated union over nine kinds: Smart Drill, Section,\r\n37:Saved set, Assignment, Guided drill, Practice set, Bootcamp day, Questions (an\r\n38:ad-hoc list), and Filter. A practice-test leg is a **Section with a run\r\n39:attached**, not its own kind: it runs, looks and closes out identically, and the\r\n40:only difference is that something is waiting on the result.\r\n41:\r\n42:Anything that has an id is named by that id alone. `?set=abc123` and not the six\r\n43:qids inside it, so editing a set cannot leave an old link running last week\'s\r\n44:questions. Only the ad-hoc list and the filter put a payload in the link, because\r\n45:they have no row to point at. Guided drills and assignments already resolve their\r\n46:own questions (`guidedDrills.ts:82`), so nothing is lost.\r\n47:\r\n48:Switches are split rather than pooled: the cross-cutting ones (back link,\r\n49:heading, which question to open on) sit alongside the kind, and the rest live\r\n50:inside the kind that owns them (sealed and timing on a Section, duration on a\r\n51:Guided drill). A switch that does not belong to its kind is **rejected** when it\r\n52:would change what the student experiences, and ignored when it is decoration. A\r\n53:sealed section that silently is not sealed is a broken measurement; a missing\r\n54:heading is not.\r\n55:\r\n56:`launchDrill()` and `parseDrillLaunch()` are a matched pair and round-trip.\r\n57:\r\n58:## Consequences\r\n59:\r\n60:- A dead link (deleted assignment, deleted set, another student\'s set) shows an\r\n61:  error with a way back, rather than falling back to a generic drill. A student\r\n62:  quietly drilling something nobody assigned is worse than a student reporting a\r\n63:  broken link.\r\n64:- An id-only link costs one row read before the drill can start, and it is the\r\n65:  read that makes the link correct.\r\n66:- The drill page is not rewritten. A small converter hands it the shape it\r\n67:  already expects, so the fix lands as a diff that can be read in one sitting.\r\n68:  The converter is temporary and says so.\r\n69:- `/drill` canonicalizes its own URL on arrival, which covers any launch path\r\n70:  missed here. Two buttons still change, because they discard identity the page\r\n71:  cannot recover: saved sets throw away the set id (`SavedSets.tsx:39`), and the\r\n72:  bootcamp day button hands over picked questions without saying it was Day 3\r\n73:  homework (`TodayView.tsx:112`).\r\n74:- Today\'s URL grammar is kept as an explicitly-marked legacy branch of the\r\n75:  parser, and never written. Assigned sections go out as links that sit in a\r\n76:  student\'s inbox for weeks; breaking one is a support message from a student.\r\n77:  The branch is deletable once those links have aged out.\r\n78:- Rejected: putting the resolved question list in every URL. It duplicates the\r\n79:  Run Record, produces links too long to send, and would have made a filter\r\n80:  drill\'s identity the questions it happened to draw rather than what the\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 5.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '1:/**\r\n2: * Resume state for every OTHER drill — Smart Drill, custom type drills, and\r\n3: * full sections.\r\n4: *\r\n5: * Guided drills and Intro to LR practice sets already survive a closed tab, a\r\n6: * sign-out, or a different device (see guidedDrillSession). Nothing else did:\r\n7: * a student mid-way through a Smart Drill who closed the browser came back to\r\n8: * a fresh shuffle, and the answers they had already given were nowhere on the\r\n9: * screen. The attempts themselves were never lost — those are written to the\r\n10: * `attempts` table per question — but the run was, which reads as lost work.\r\n11: *\r\n12: * This rides the exact same store as the other two, namespaced so the three can\r\n13: * never collide:\r\n14: *\r\n15: *     practice set   →  pool_slug "mc-practice-1"\r\n16: *     guided drill   →  pool_slug "guided::<assignmentId>"\r\n17: *     bootcamp day   →  pool_slug "drill::bootcamp-day::<day>::<part>"\r\n18: *     everything else→  pool_slug "drill::<mode>::<fingerprint>"\r\n19: *\r\n20: * Failure policy, inherited: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must\r\n21: * never block starting a drill), writes surface to the caller.\r\n22: */\r\n23:\r\n24:import {\r\n25:  loadPracticeSession,\r\n26:  savePracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n27:  completePracticeSession,\r\n28:  type PracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n29:} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/practiceSessionStore\';\r\n30:import type { PracticeSession } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\r\n31:\r\n32:export const DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX = \'drill::\';\r\n33:\r\n34:export type DrillResumeSession = PracticeSession;\r\n35:export type DrillResumeSnapshot = PracticeSessionSnapshot;\r\n36:\r\n37:export type DrillResumeIdentity = {\r\n38:  kind: \'bootcamp-day\';\r\n39:  day: number;\r\n40:  part: \'homework\' | \'practice\';\r\n41:};\r\n42:\r\n43:/** Short, stable, filename-safe digest of whatever identifies the run. */\r\n44:function fingerprint(input: string): string {\r\n45:  let h = 2166136261;\r\n46:  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\r\n47:    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n48:    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);\r\n49:  }\r\n50:  return (h >>> 0).toString(36);\r\n51:}\r\n52:\r\n53:/**\r\n54: * Key one run\'s resume row.\r\n55: *\r\n56: * A full section is identified by the section itself; a filtered drill by the\r\n57: * filters that drew it; Smart Drill by nothing but its mode, because it has a\r\n58: * single continuous run per student. Returns null for the surfaces that own\r\n59: * their own resume rows (guided, practice sets) and for anything launched from\r\n60: * an explicit list of qids, where the caller already knows the set.\r\n61: */\r\n62:export function drillResumeKey(input: {\r\n63:  mode: string;\r\n64:  config?: unknown;\r\n65:  identity?: DrillResumeIdentity;\r\n66:}): string | null {\r\n67:  if (input.identity?.kind === \'bootcamp-day\') {\r\n68:    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}bootcamp-day::${input.identity.day}::${input.identity.part}`;\r\n69:  }\r\n70:\r\n71:  const mode = input.mode;\r\n72:  if (mode === \'adaptive\') return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}adaptive`;\r\n73:\r\n74:  const config = (input.config ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;\r\n75:\r\n76:  if (mode === \'full-section\') {\r\n77:    if (!config) return null;\r\n78:    const pt = config.pt;\r\n79:    const section = config.section;\r\n80:    if (pt == null || section == null) return null;\r\n81:    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::pt${pt}-s${section}`;\r\n82:  }\r\n83:\r\n84:  if (mode === \'type-drill\' || mode === \'natural-drill\') {\r\n85:    if (!config) return null;\r\n86:    const selected = config.selectedQids as string[] | undefined;\r\n87:    if (selected?.length) {\r\n88:      // A pre-selected set is its own identity: the same list re-opened is the\r\n89:      // same run, a different list is a different one.\r\n90:      return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::set-${fingerprint([...selected].sort().join(\',\'))}`;\r\n91:    }\r\n92:    const shape = JSON.stringify({\r\n93:      qtypes: config.qtypes ?? [],\r\n94:      difficulties: config.difficulties ?? [],\r\n95:      pts: config.pts ?? [],\r\n96:      sections: config.sections ?? [],\r\n97:      qfeatures: config.qfeatures ?? [],\r\n98:      reasoningTypes: config.reasoningTypes ?? [],\r\n99:      searchQuery: config.searchQuery ?? \'\',\r\n100:      questionNumber: config.questionNumber ?? null,\r\n101:      count: config.count ?? null,\r\n102:    });\r\n103:    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::${fingerprint(shape)}`;\r\n104:  }\r\n105:\r\n106:  return null;\r\n107:}\r\n108:\r\n109:/** The stored run for this drill, or null when there is nothing to resume. */\r\n110:export async function loadDrillResumeSession(\r\n111:  userId: string,\r\n112:  key: string | null,\r\n113:  legacyKey?: string | null,\r\n114:): Promise<DrillResumeSession | null> {\r\n115:  if (!userId || !key) return null;\r\n116:  const current = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);\r\n117:  if (current || !legacyKey || legacyKey === key) return current;\r\n118:  return loadPracticeSession(userId, legacyKey);\r\n119:}\r\n120:\r\n121:/** Write where the student is right now (local-first, debounced cloud upsert). */\r\n122:export function saveDrillResumeSnapshot(\r\n123:  userId: string,\r\n124:  key: string | null,\r\n125:  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\r\n126:): Promise<void> {\r\n127:  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();\r\n128:  return savePracticeSessionSnapshot(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\r\n129:}\r\n130:\r\n131:/**\r\n132: * Mark the run finished. Nothing is deleted — the queue and every answer stay\r\n133: * in the row for review — it simply stops being offered as a resume, so the\r\n134: * next launch of the same drill draws fresh questions.\r\n135: */\r\n136:export function completeDrillResumeSession(\r\n137:  userId: string,\r\n138:  key: string | null,\r\n139:  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\r\n140:): Promise<void> {\r\n141:  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();\r\n142:  return completePracticeSession(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\r\n143:}\r\n144:\r\n145:/**\r\n146: * Is this stored row worth resuming?\r\n147: *\r\n148: * Unlike a guided drill, these runs are drawn fresh on every launch, so there\r\n149: * is no fixed set to compare against — the stored queue IS the run. It is\r\n150: * resumable when it is still in progress and the student actually answered\r\n151: * something; a row with no answers has nothing to restore and would only\r\n152: * freeze a stale shuffle in place.\r\n153: */\r\n154:export function isResumableDrillSession(stored: DrillResumeSession | null): boolean {\r\n155:  return (\r\n156:    !!stored &&\r\n157:    stored.status === \'in_progress\' &&\r\n158:    stored.question_queue.length > 0 &&\r\n159:    stored.attempts.length > 0\r\n160:  );\r\n161:}\r\n162:\r\n163:\r\n164:\r\n165:\r\n166:\r\n167:\r\n168:\r\n169:\r\n170:\r\n171:\r\n172:\r\n173:\r\n174:\r\n175:\r\n176:\r\n177:\r\n178:\r\n179:\r\n180:\r\nsupabase/migrations\\20260412201001_f0d90061-861a-4c44-92d4-67e77899481b.sql:4:-- Create question_views table\r\nsupabase/migrations\\20260412201001_f0d90061-861a-4c44-92d4-67e77899481b.sql:5:CREATE TABLE public.question_views (\r\nsupabase/migrations\\20260412201001_f0d90061-861a-4c44-92d4-67e77899481b.sql:15:ALTER TABLE public.question_views ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\r\nsupabase/migrations\\20260412201001_f0d90061-861a-4c44-92d4-67e77899481b.sql:19:ON public.question_views FOR SELECT\r\nsupabase/migrations\\20260412201001_f0d90061-861a-4c44-92d4-67e77899481b.sql:24:ON public.question_views FOR INSERT\r\nsupabase/migrations\\20260412201001_f0d90061-861a-4c44-92d4-67e77899481b.sql:29:ON public.question_views FOR UPDATE\r\nsupabase/migrations\\20260412201001_f0d90061-861a-4c44-92d4-67e77899481b.sql:35:CREATE INDEX idx_question_views_class_qid ON public.question_views (class_id, qid);\r\nsupabase/migrations\\20260412201001_f0d90061-861a-4c44-92d4-67e77899481b.sql:36:CREATE INDEX idx_question_views_class_mode ON public.question_views (class_id, mode);\r\nsupabase/migrations\\20260811141136_1acff12f-1082-4f28-b32e-c99d56b997f5.sql:3:  AND table_name = \'practice_sessions\'\r\nsupabase/migrations\\20260829220000_admin_reads_student_work.sql:34:    \'question_views\',\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 5.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '/**\r\n * Practice-set resume store for the Intro to LR practice drills.\r\n *\r\n * A student who starts a practice set and leaves — closes the tab, clicks away\r\n * to a lesson, signs out, comes back tomorrow on another machine — must land\r\n * back on the question they stopped on, with every answer they already gave\r\n * still marked. React state and URL params cannot do that: both die with the\r\n * page. So each practice set keeps one durable row per user in the shared\r\n * bootcamp_kv persistence (the same layer module_progress uses), written on\r\n * every answer and on every question move.\r\n *\r\n * These are standalone functions rather than a hook because the drill runner\r\n * (src/pages/Drill.tsx) lives outside the bootcamp\'s React tree — the same\r\n * reason recordPracticeCompletion.ts exists.\r\n *\r\n * Failure policy: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must never block\r\n * starting a drill). Writes throw so the caller can surface a toast rather than\r\n * silently losing a student\'s place.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport { getPersistence } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/factory\';\r\nimport { TABLES, PracticeSession, type PracticeSessionAttempt } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\r\nimport { now } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/ids\';\r\nimport {\r\n  MAIN_CONCLUSION_PRACTICE_POOLS,\r\n  type PracticePool,\r\n} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/data/practiceDrillPools\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Deterministic per-user, per-pool record id, so every read and write lands on\r\n * the same row without a lookup. Mirrors progressIdFor\'s FNV-1a approach but\r\n * hashes user + slug together.\r\n */\r\nexport function practiceSessionIdFor(userId: string, poolSlug: string): string {\r\n  const input = `${userId}::${poolSlug}`;\r\n  let h = 0x811c9dc5;\r\n  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\r\n    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193);\r\n  }\r\n  let h2 = 0x811c9dc5;\r\n  for (let i = input.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\r\n    h2 ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n    h2 = Math.imul(h2, 0x01000193);\r\n  }\r\n  const hex = (h >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, \'0\');\r\n  const hex2 = (h2 >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, \'0\');\r\n  return `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${hex}${hex2.slice(0, 4)}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface PracticeSessionSnapshot {\r\n  questionQueue: string[];\r\n  currentIndex: number;\r\n  attempts: PracticeSessionAttempt[];\r\n  /** Section clock already spent, for the surfaces that run one. */\r\n  elapsedMs?: number;\r\n  /** Answers that were already down when the clock ran out, if it did. */\r\n  regulationAnsweredQids?: string[];\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Read the stored session for one pool. Returns null when there is nothing to\r\n * resume, when the row is corrupt, or when persistence is unavailable — all of\r\n * which mean "start fresh", never "block the student".\r\n */\r\nexport async function loadPracticeSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  poolSlug: string,\r\n): Promise<PracticeSession | null> {\r\n  if (!userId || !poolSlug) return null;\r\n  try {\r\n    const persist = getPersistence(userId);\r\n    // Wait for cloud hydration first, otherwise a cold load on a second device\r\n    // reports "nothing to resume" while the row is still in flight.\r\n    await persist.ready();\r\n    const raw = await persist.get<unknown>(TABLES.practice_sessions, practiceSessionIdFor(userId, poolSlug));\r\n    if (!raw) return null;\r\n    const parsed = PracticeSession.safeParse(raw);\r\n    if (!parsed.success) {\r\n      console.warn(\'[practice-session] stored row failed validation, ignoring\', {\r\n        poolSlug,\r\n        issues: parsed.error.issues,\r\n      });\r\n      return null;\r\n    }\r\n    return parsed.data;\r\n  } catch (e) {\r\n    console.warn(\'[practice-session] load failed, starting fresh\', { poolSlug, error: e });\r\n    return null;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Read the stored session for every practice pool, for the index UI. */\r\nexport async function loadAllPracticeSessions(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n): Promise<Record<string, PracticeSession>> {\r\n  const slugs = Object.keys(MAIN_CONCLUSION_PRACTICE_POOLS);\r\n  const rows = await Promise.all(slugs.map((slug) => loadPracticeSession(userId, slug)));\r\n  const out: Record<string, PracticeSession> = {};\r\n  rows.forEach((row, i) => {\r\n    if (row) out[slugs[i]] = row;\r\n  });\r\n  return out;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Write the student\'s current place in a practice set. Called after every\r\n * answer and every question move, so it must stay cheap — the persistence layer\r\n * writes to the local cache synchronously and debounces the Supabase upsert.\r\n *\r\n * A completed row is never downgraded back to in_progress by a snapshot write;\r\n * re-running a finished set starts a fresh row via resetPracticeSession.\r\n */\r\nexport async function savePracticeSessionSnapshot(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  pool: Pick<PracticePool, \'slug\' | \'completionId\'>,\r\n  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !pool?.slug) return;\r\n  if (snapshot.questionQueue.length === 0) return;\r\n'}]

## assistant

Smart Drill is partly wired, but the adaptive loop is not internally coherent yet. The main CTA works. Several other entry points and the analytics pipeline do not.

### Current path

- The live hub is `/classroom/drill`, mounted in `src/App.tsx:502-510`. `/foyer` is now only a redirect at `src/App.tsx:280-281`, despite older docs naming it as primary.
- The main LR CTA is “Practice your weakest types” in `src/pages/AcademyMainhub.tsx:135-143,249-278`. It navigates to `/drill` with `{ mode: "adaptive" }`.
- `src/components/drill/DrillLaunchGate.tsx:67-104,164-174` converts that route state into `?drill=smart-drill`.
- `src/lib/drillRunSpec.ts:101-104` maps it to adaptive mode.
- `/drill` is protected and rendered through the gate at `src/App.tsx:430-432`.
- The default runner uses `OfficialSmartDrillBar` at `src/pages/Drill.tsx:4196-4216`. Submit persists the answer, Next triggers another selection, Skip excludes that question for the current session, and End returns to Classroom.

### Concrete defects

1. **A visible practice button is dead.**  
   `src/components/mainhub/insightSlides.ts:48-63` gives “Practice today” the bare URL `/drill`. `src/components/mainhub/PracticeInsightStage.tsx:55-68` navigates there without mode/state. The gate supplies an empty spec, then `src/pages/Drill.tsx:509-514` redirects back to `/classroom`. It appears to do nothing.

2. **Pool settings are calculated, then ignored.**  
   Smart Drill filters a pool using `question_usage`, allow-repeats, prefer-unseen, and recycle settings at `src/pages/Drill.tsx:581-634`. The actual selector later discards that pool and calls `questionBank.getAllQuestions()` at `src/pages/Drill.tsx:1130`. It therefore ignores the displayed pool status and those user settings.

3. **Current-session answers are double-counted.**  
   Submit adds the answer to the engine at `src/pages/Drill.tsx:2056-2065`. The next selection combines `adaptiveEngine.getRecentAttempts()` with all `session.attempts` again at `src/pages/Drill.tsx:1131-1148`. Recent answers receive duplicate statistical weight.

4. **The default Smart Drill does not log misses to the Wrong Answer Journal.**  
   The official layout calls `commitCurrentAdaptiveAnswer` at `src/pages/Drill.tsx:4196-4207`. That function logs correct revisits at `src/pages/Drill.tsx:2071-2100`, but never calls `logWrongAnswer`. End merely navigates at `src/pages/Drill.tsx:2878-2885,4214`. The old `?layout=classic` flow uses different submission code, so behavior depends on layout.

5. **The implemented selector forbids redemption questions.**  
   `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:438-453` removes every historically attempted qid, regardless of whether the latest answer was wrong. The test explicitly says redemption is deferred at `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.test.ts:360-378`. This conflicts with the dormant 40/40/20 algorithm’s promised 40% redemption at `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:819-831`.

6. **There are three adaptive implementations, but only one real path.**  
   The live runner uses `selectSmartDrillQuestion` at `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:431-537`. The 40/40/20 `generateSmartDrill` at `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:833-1030` is called only from the unimported `src/components/drill/TypeDrillPicker.tsx:184-227`. The older `selectNextQuestion` is test-only. This duplication makes the product description and actual behavior diverge.

7. **Sparse history is over-trusted.**  
   `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:417-428` labels fewer than 15 attempts “cold,” but the selector still treats one miss as 100% weakness at `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:466-504`. There is no minimum sample size or shrinkage toward a neutral prior.

8. **Cold-start selection is deterministic and bank-order biased.**  
   With no history, candidates receive equal scores. Strict `>` tie handling at `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:523` selects the first matching bank item. The dormant generator separately uses biased `sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5)` shuffles at `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:849,930`, already flagged in `COMPREHENSIVEAUDIT.md:1103-1108`.

9. **Adaptive state can leak across account changes.**  
   `src/contexts/AdaptiveEngineContext.tsx:5-14` creates one engine for the app lifetime. It is mounted once at `src/App.tsx:223-230`, not keyed by user. `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:139-147` refuses hydration once hydrated or failed and retains prior attempts. Signing into another account without a full remount can use the previous account’s performance profile.

10. **A transient history failure disables personalization for the app lifetime.**  
    Failure is marked permanently at `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:154-200`. The independent audit documents the same defect at `COMPREHENSIVEAUDIT.md:708-713`.

11. **Resume semantics are broken for an infinite adaptive stream.**  
    Adaptive initializes `session.questionQueue` to essentially the entire pool at `src/pages/Drill.tsx:542-543,691`, while `currentIndex` stays zero. Last activity stores that queue/index at `src/pages/Drill.tsx:1055-1108`. Its guard ignores `currentQuestion.qid`, so it refuses to update when only the selected adaptive question changes. `src/components/mainhub/useTodayFocus.ts:279-295` can consequently report thousands of questions “left” and stale resume metadata.

12. **Selection decisions cannot be audited.**  
    The selector computes a reason and matched weaknesses at `src/lib/adaptiveEngine.ts:523-533`, but `src/pages/Drill.tsx:1149-1154` discards everything except the question. Selection is fully client-side. No Supabase function selects questions, and no row records why a question was served.

### Entry-point inventory

- Working main entry: `src/pages/AcademyMainhub.tsx:135-143,249-278`.
- Broken secondary entry: `src/components/mainhub/insightSlides.ts:48-63`.
- Bare `/drill` resource links also exist in:
  - `supabase/functions/teacher-operator-chat/index.ts:35`
  - `supabase/functions/teacher-link-search/index.ts:31`
  - `supabase/migrations/20260620221500_platform_resource_index_backfill.sql:11`
  - `supabase/migrations/20260620222414_cda93b64-f5ac-41f7-8ae0-389cfeb469c3.sql:7`
- `ModeSelector` still contains a “Start drill” adaptive button at `src/components/drill/ModeSelector.tsx:95-109`, but it has no importer.
- `TypeDrillPicker` contains the separate “Build Smart Drill” flow at `src/components/drill/TypeDrillPicker.tsx:318-343`, but it also has no importer.
- The auth landing page promises “No busywork, no random sets” at `src/components/auth-landing/academy.tsx:384-386`, which the current cold-start behavior does not reliably uphold.

### Data involved

- `attempts`: answer, correctness, type, level, time, mode, selected answer. Generated shape at `src/integrations/supabase/types.ts:594-678`.
- `question_exposures`: every served qid, newest 500 used for freshness. Service at `src/lib/questionExposure.ts:13-54`; schema/RLS at `supabase/migrations/20260806180100_question_exposures.sql:15-39`.
- `question_usage`: per-mode seen count and timestamp. Service at `src/lib/questionPoolService.ts:44-135`; atomic increment RPC at `supabase/migrations/20260709120000_phase2_atomic_counters.sql:43-58`.
- `question_views`: displayed/answered telemetry, used at `src/lib/questionPoolService.ts:62-99`.
- Resume state rides the bootcamp persistence layer through `src/lib/drillResumeSession.ts:53-160`.

### Recommended implementation boundary

- Make one pure, authoritative Smart Drill selector.
- Key profile hydration by user/class and permit retry after transient failure.
- Feed the selector the actual filtered eligible pool.
- Deduplicate attempt history before profiling.
- Use sample-size-aware mastery estimates.
- Define a real loop: weak-skill progression, delayed redemption, maintenance, and controlled exploration. Do not immediately repeat the same missed qid.
- Persist selection reason and profile snapshot for diagnosis.
- Give adaptive mode its own continuous-session resume semantics.
- Route every Smart Drill entry through one canonical `launchDrill({ kind: "smart-drill" })` helper.
- Unify answer persistence and WAJ logging across official and classic layouts.

No active source or product document mentions LSAT Demon by name, so its exact selection policy is not represented locally.

Verification run: 40 focused tests passed across the adaptive engine, launch gate, Smart Drill bar, and Mainhub. Coverage is still missing for the actual `Drill.tsx` adaptive sequence, user switching, exposure service, the “Practice today” click, wrong-answer journaling, and resume behavior. No files were edited.
